# Remaining Packages

Packages from the MVP list not yet available in ekapkgs (excluding language ecosystem packages).

**Original total: 9796 packages | Ported: 4024 (41.1%) | Remaining: 5772**

Last updated: 2026-08-15

| Category | Original | Ported | Remaining | % Done |
|----------|----------|--------|-----------|--------|
| CLI Utilities | 7035 | 2884 | 4151 | 40% |
| Libraries (general) | 548 | 386 | 162 | 70% |
| Qt/KDE Stack | 336 | 0 | 336 | 0% |
| Build Systems & Compilers | 305 | 159 | 146 | 52% |
| Science & Math | 257 | 60 | 197 | 23% |
| Fonts & Themes | 230 | 122 | 108 | 53% |
| Desktop Environments | 149 | 15 | 134 | 10% |
| Networking | 132 | 77 | 55 | 58% |
| Text Processing | 98 | 35 | 63 | 35% |
| Version Control | 96 | 55 | 41 | 57% |
| Audio/Video | 80 | 35 | 45 | 43% |
| Compression | 78 | 34 | 44 | 43% |
| Security | 69 | 34 | 35 | 49% |
| X.org / Wayland | 67 | 6 | 61 | 8% |
| Embedded & Hardware | 65 | 33 | 32 | 50% |
| GUI Applications | 53 | 5 | 48 | 9% |
| Servers & Daemons | 38 | 20 | 18 | 52% |
| Databases | 33 | 9 | 24 | 27% |
| GTK/GNOME Stack | 21 | 12 | 9 | 57% |

| Graphics & Imaging | 18 | 11 | 7 | 61% |

| GStreamer | 11 | 0 | 11 | 0% |

## Completed Infrastructure

- **gtk4**: Override in `top-level.nix` (Vulkan disabled). Built and cached in nix store.
- **wrapGAppsHook4**: Defined in `top-level.nix` as override of `wrapGAppsNoGuiHook` with gtk4. Functional.
- **libadwaita**: Committed at version 1.9.2.
- **GStreamer**: Ported as `pkgs.gstreamer` scope with core, base, good, bad, ugly plugins. Legacy `gst_all_1` alias in `top-level.nix`.
- **PulseAudio**: Ported at 17.0 with `libpulseaudio` library-only alias.
- **PipeWire**: Ported at 1.6.8.
- **JACK2**: Ported at 1.9.22 with `libjack2` library-only alias.
- **fftwFloat**: Alias for `fftw.override { precision = "single"; }`.
- **makeDesktopItem / copyDesktopItems**: Build support infrastructure.
- **CUDA packages**: Now viable via `pkgsCuda` scope from core-pkgs.

## Top Blockers

These missing dependencies in core-pkgs block the most remaining packages:

| Dependency | Packages Blocked |
|------------|-----------------|
| `kdePackages` | 121 |
| `openal` | 65 |
| `blueprint-compiler` | 64 |
| `wxwidgets_3_2` | 60 |
| `qt6Packages` | 60 |
| `webkitgtk_4_1` | 56 |
| `xorg-server` | 54 |
| `pandoc` | 50 |
| `installFonts` | 46 |
| `libpq` | 46 |

Adding `wrapGAppsHook4` + `libadwaita` would unblock ~385 GNOME/GTK4 apps.
Adding `libpulseaudio` + `pipewire` + `libjack2` would unblock ~370 audio packages.
Adding `kdePackages` + `qt6Packages` would unblock ~180 KDE/Qt6 apps.
Adding `gst_all_1` would unblock ~149 GStreamer-based multimedia packages.
